// FUNC 180013fe0 __alloca_probe
unsigned __int64 __fastcall _alloca_probe()
{
  unsigned __int64 result; // rax
  char *v1; // r10
  char *StackLimit; // r11
  char v3; // [rsp+18h] [rbp+8h] BYREF

  v1 = &v3 - result;
  if ( (unsigned __int64)&v3 < result )
    v1 = nullptr;
  StackLimit = (char *)NtCurrentTeb()->NtTib.StackLimit;
  if ( v1 < StackLimit )
  {
    LOWORD(v1) = (unsigned __int16)v1 & 0xF000;
    do
    {
      StackLimit -= 4096;
      *StackLimit = 0;
    }
    while ( v1 != StackLimit );
  }
  return result;
}
// ENDFUNC
// FUNC 180014030 __GSHandlerCheck
__int64 __fastcall _GSHandlerCheck(__int64 a1, __int64 a2, __int64 a3, __int64 a4)
{
  _GSHandlerCheckCommon(a2, a4, *(_QWORD *)(a4 + 56));
  return 1;
}
// ENDFUNC
// FUNC 180014050 __GSHandlerCheckCommon
__int64 __fastcall _GSHandlerCheckCommon(__int64 a1, __int64 a2)
{
  __int64 v2; // rcx
  __int64 result; // rax

  v2 = *(unsigned int *)(*(_QWORD *)(a2 + 16) + 8LL);
  result = *(_QWORD *)(a2 + 8);
  if ( (*(_BYTE *)(v2 + result + 3) & 0xF) != 0 )
    return *(_BYTE *)(v2 + result + 3) & 0xF0;
  return result;
}
// ENDFUNC
// FUNC 1800140c0 __security_check_cookie
void __cdecl _security_check_cookie(uintptr_t StackCookie)
{
  __int64 v1; // rcx

  if ( StackCookie != _security_cookie )
ReportFailure:
    _report_gsfailure(StackCookie);
  v1 = __ROL8__(StackCookie, 16);
  if ( (_WORD)v1 != 0 )
  {
    StackCookie = __ROR8__(v1, 16);
    goto ReportFailure;
  }
}
// ENDFUNC
// FUNC 1800140e0 ?dllmain_crt_dispatch@@YAHQEAUHINSTANCE__@@KQEAX@Z
__int64 __fastcall dllmain_crt_dispatch(HINSTANCE a1, int a2, void *const a3)
{
  int v3; // edx
  __int64 v4; // rdx
  __int64 result; // rax

  if ( a2 == 0 )
    return dllmain_crt_process_detach(a3 != nullptr);
